Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electrical object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 184. The moduli space has dimension at most 455 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on electrical structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 184. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 455. QED.
